### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_multi_turn_wiring.py::TestOverlayPassesConfigCheck::test_real_campaign_overlay_loads_via_config_system`  (0.0216s)

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_real_campaign_overlay_loads_via_config_system(self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    runner = CampaignRunner(_campaign(tmp_path))
    experiment = ExperimentConfig(
        name="exp_x",
        agent_config={"agents": ["baseline"], "multi_turn": {"timeout_seconds": 60}},
        evaluation_config={"per_segment": 3},
        repetition_count=1,
    )
    output_dir = tmp_path / "campaign_output"
    plans = runner._multi_turn_plans([experiment], output_dir)
    # Both an enabled run (param dict opt-in under "all") and a disabled
    # run must survive the fail-closed overlay key check.
    for frag in (plans[(0, 1)], None):
        overlay = runner._build_config_overlay(experiment, 1, multi_turn=frag)
        overlay_path = tmp_path / f"overlay_{frag is not None}.yaml"
        overlay_path.write_text(
            yaml.safe_dump(overlay, sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        monkeypatch.setenv("CONFIG_OVERLAY_PATH", str(overlay_path))
        cfg = load_config("agent/agent_eval")["agent_eval"]
        assert cfg["campaign_id"] == "mt_campaign"
        assert cfg["agents"] == ["baseline"]
        assert cfg["multi_turn"]["enabled"] is (frag is not None)
        if frag is not None:
            assert cfg["multi_turn"]["timeout_seconds"] == 60
            assert cfg["multi_turn"]["output_dir"].endswith("exp_x_run1")
```

```python
def test_only_selects_by_grid_position_ids(tmp_path):
    """--only 1,3 runs exactly exp_1_* and exp_3_*; names are NOT renumbered."""
    campaign = _grid_campaign(tmp_path)
    experiments = campaign.expand_parameter_grid()
    names = [e.name for e in experiments]
    assert names[0].startswith("exp_1_") and names[-1].startswith("exp_3_")

    selected, skipped = CampaignRunner._select_experiments(experiments, ["1", "exp_3"])
    assert [e.name for e in selected] == [names[0], names[2]]
    assert skipped == [names[1]]
```

```python
def test_subset_run_only_touches_selected(tmp_path):
    """Skipped experiments get no _run_experiment call and no output dir."""
    campaign = _grid_campaign(tmp_path)
    calls: list[str] = []
    original = CampaignRunner._run_experiment
    CampaignRunner._run_experiment = _fake_run_factory(calls)
    try:
        result = CampaignRunner(campaign, git_sha="sha_subset").run_campaign(only=["2"])
    finally:
        CampaignRunner._run_experiment = original

    assert calls == [e.name for e in campaign.expand_parameter_grid() if e.name.startswith("exp_2_")]
    out = Path(campaign.output_dir)
    for d in out.iterdir():
        if d.is_dir():
            assert d.name.startswith("exp_2_")
    assert result.subset["comparisons"] == "skipped_subset_run"
    assert len(result.subset["skipped"]) == 2
    assert result.comparisons == {}
    # new runs carry the git_sha of this run
    assert all(r.git_sha == "sha_subset" for r in result.runs)
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/campaign/test_only_subset.py::test_subset_run_merges_previous_result`  (0.0188s)

_campaign_result.json keeps skipped entries, preserves comparisons, backs up._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_subset_run_merges_previous_result(tmp_path):
    """campaign_result.json keeps skipped entries, preserves comparisons, backs up."""
    campaign = _grid_campaign(tmp_path)
    out = Path(campaign.output_dir)
    out.mkdir(parents=True)
    # Previous full-run result uses the REAL positional names: exp_1/exp_3 done,
    # exp_2 failed (the campaign_001 rerun scenario).
    real_names = [e.name for e in campaign.expand_parameter_grid()]
    n1 = [n for n in real_names if n.startswith("exp_1_")][0]
    n2 = [n for n in real_names if n.startswith("exp_2_")][0]
    n3 = [n for n in real_names if n.startswith("exp_3_")][0]
    prev = CampaignResult(
        campaign_id=campaign.campaign_id,
        campaign_hash="oldhash",
        runs=[
            ExperimentRun(experiment_name=n1, run_number=1, output_dir="o1", success=True,
                          metrics={"task_success_rate": 0.5}),
            ExperimentRun(experiment_name=n2, run_number=1, output_dir="o2", success=False,
                          error="timed out"),
            ExperimentRun(experiment_name=n3, run_number=1, output_dir="o3", success=True,
                          metrics={"task_success_rate": 0.7}),
        ],
        comparisons={f"{n1}_vs_{n3}": {"status": "success"}},
        summary={"total_runs": 3},
    )
    (out / "campaign_result.json").write_text(json.dumps(prev.to_dict()), encoding="utf-8")

    calls: list[str] = []

    def fake_run(self, experiment, run_number, output_dir, multi_turn=None):
        calls.append(experiment.name)
        return ExperimentRun(
            experiment_name=experiment.name,
            run_number=run_number,
            output_dir=str(output_dir / experiment.name / f"run_{run_number}"),
            success=True,
            metrics={"task_success_rate": 0.9},
            git_sha=self.git_sha,
        )

    original = CampaignRunner._run_experiment
    CampaignRunner._run_experiment = fake_run
    try:
        result = CampaignRunner(campaign, git_sha="sha2").run_campaign(only=["2"])
    finally:
        CampaignRunner._run_experiment = original

    on_disk = json.loads((out / "campaign_result.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    by_name = {r["experiment_name"]: r for r in on_disk["runs"]}
    assert len(on_disk["runs"]) == 3
    # skipped exp_1/exp_3 keep OLD entries verbatim (old metrics, no git_sha)
    assert by_name[n1]["metrics"]["task_success_rate"] == 0.5 and by_name[n1]["git_sha"] == ""
    assert by_name[n3]["metrics"]["task_success_rate"] == 0.7
    # re-run exp_2 entry REPLACED by the new successful run
    assert by_name[n2]["success"] is True
    assert by_name[n2]["metrics"]["task_success_rate"] == 0.9
    assert by_name[n2]["git_sha"] == "sha2"
    # comparisons preserved from previous full run
    assert on_disk["comparisons"] == {f"{n1}_vs_{n3}": {"status": "success"}}
    # summary recomputed over merged runs (3 entries, 3 successful now)
    assert on_disk["summary"]["total_runs"] == 3
    assert on_disk["summary"]["successful_runs"] == 3
    # subset metadata + backup file
    assert on_disk["subset"]["only"] == ["2"]
    assert on_disk["subset"]["skipped"] == [n1, n3]
    assert list(out.glob("campaign_result_backup_*.json"))
```

```python
def test_full_run_without_only_unchanged(tmp_path):
    """Regression: no --only -> all experiments run, auto comparisons, no subset field."""
    campaign = _grid_campaign(tmp_path)
    calls: list[str] = []
    original = CampaignRunner._run_experiment
    CampaignRunner._run_experiment = _fake_run_factory(calls)
    try:
        result = CampaignRunner(campaign).run_campaign()
    finally:
        CampaignRunner._run_experiment = original

    assert len(calls) == 3
    assert len(result.runs) == 3
    assert result.subset == {}
    assert result.comparisons  # auto-generated pairs vs first experiment still work
```

```python
def test_step_count_match_catches_phantom_step(evaluator):
    """exec_018 shape: empty plan must produce zero steps — a phantom step
    keeps every legacy metric at 1.0 but must fail step_count_match."""
    exp = {"all_steps_ok": True, "step_error_kinds": [], "bad_reference_count": 0,
           "num_steps": 0, "step_outputs": []}
    phantom = {"steps": [{"tool": "FilterTool", "ok": True, "args": {},
                          "data": {"candidates": []}, "error_kind": None}]}
    m = evaluator.score_component(phantom, exp)
    assert m["step_count_match"] == 0.0
    assert m["all_steps_ok"] == 1.0  # legacy metric alone would NOT catch it
```

```python
def test_real_tool_passes_all_cases(cases, evaluator):
    """Every correctness metric must be 1.0 (step_success_rate is a raw
    rate and legitimately <1.0 on expected-failure cases)."""
    bad = []
    for c in cases:
        m = evaluator.score_component(evaluator.run_component(c.input), c.expected)
        fails = {k: m[k] for k in CORRECTNESS_METRICS if k in m and m[k] != 1.0}
        if fails:
            bad.append((c.case_id, fails))
    assert bad == [], f"cases not at 1.0: {bad}"
```

```python
def test_grounding_benchmark_with_fake_jev():
    """Test grounding benchmark end-to-end with mocked Jev provider."""
    # Set fake API key for config expansion
    os.environ["TYPESAFE_API_KEY"] = "fake_test_key"

    with patch("httpx.Client") as mock_client_class:
        mock_client = Mock()
        mock_client_class.return_value = mock_client

        # Mock Jev response
        mock_response = Mock()
        mock_response.status_code = 200
        mock_response.json.return_value = {
            "answers": {
                "claim_0": {"noul": 0.9, "confidence": 0.95},
                "claim_1": {"noul": 0.8, "confidence": 0.9},
            },
            "usage": {"input_tokens": 10, "output_tokens": 5},
        }
        mock_client.post.return_value = mock_response

        benchmark = GroundingBenchmark(config={"use_jev": True})

        case_input = {
            "case_id": "grounding_001",
            "input": {
                "user_request": "I want an action game",
                "evidence": [
                    {"item_id": "vg_001", "title": "Action Game", "main_category": "Action", "price": 30.0}
                ],
            },
            "expected": {
                "claims_supported": 1,
                "total_claims": 1,
                "grounding_rate": 1.0,
                "hallucination_rate": 0.0,
            },
        }

        actual_output = benchmark.run_component(case_input["input"])
        metrics = benchmark.score_component(actual_output, case_input["expected"])

        # Verify Jev was called and produced results
        assert "jev_result" in actual_output
        assert actual_output["jev_result"]["evaluation_state"] == "success"
        assert actual_output["jev_result"]["total_claims"] > 0  # Realistic answer produces claims

        # Verify metrics are computed
        assert metrics["total_claims"] > 0
        assert 0.0 <= metrics["grounding_rate"] <= 1.0
```

### [PASSED] `src/tests/evaluation/component/benchmarks/test_grounding_phase1.py::test_partial_judge_error_counted_separately`  (0.0023s)

_Faithfulness batch fails but communication succeeds -> judge_error metric, run ok._

**Test source** (assertions = expected values)

```python
def test_partial_judge_error_counted_separately(monkeypatch):
    """Faithfulness batch fails but communication succeeds -> judge_error metric, run ok."""
    _patched_judge_config(monkeypatch)
    monkeypatch.delenv("TYPESAFE_API_KEY", raising=False)

    with patch("httpx.Client") as cls:
        client = Mock()
        cls.return_value = client
        client.post.side_effect = [
            Exception("jev down"),
            _resp({"relevance": {"score": 3}}),
        ]
        bench = GroundingBenchmark(config=_config())
        out = bench.run_component(_case_input())

    assert out["evidence_kind"] == "real_inference_output"  # Phase 4.3: real compose_answer output
    jev_result = out["jev_result"]
    assert jev_result["evaluation_state"] == "partial_error"
    assert jev_result["judge_error_count"] == 1

    metrics = bench.score_component(out, {})
    assert metrics["judge_error_count"] == 1
    # unsupported_count must equal the number of verdict=="unsupported" claims —
    # judge_error claims are excluded from both supported and unsupported counts.
    n_unsupported = sum(1 for c in jev_result["claims"] if c["verdict"] == "unsupported")
    assert metrics["unsupported_count"] == n_unsupported
    assert metrics["hallucination_rate"] == pytest.approx(
        n_unsupported / max(n_unsupported + jev_result["supported_count"], 1) if (n_unsupported + jev_result["supported_count"]) else 0.0
    )
```

```python
def test_label_agreement_partial_and_zero():
    """A wrong expected label must be caught: agreement < 1, and 0 when all wrong."""
    bench = GroundingBenchmark(config=_config())
    # Jev says supported; expected disagrees on 2 of the 3 asserted keys
    expected = {"claims_supported": 1, "grounding_rate": 1.0, "hallucination_rate": 1.0}
    metrics = bench.score_component(_actual_output(), expected)
    assert metrics["label_agreement"] == pytest.approx(2 / 3)
    all_wrong = {"claims_supported": 0, "grounding_rate": 0.0, "hallucination_rate": 1.0}
    assert bench.score_component(_actual_output(), all_wrong)["label_agreement"] == 0.0
```
